""" Let's have 4 real examples of users' comments.
Great deal , fast shipping . Would be happy to order again if I need to. 

Came in with a broken faceplate and only worked 8 hours before it froze and couldn’t play the graphics for the game. This was not the Microsoft graphics card and not the processor either. The system can’t handle the games. I tried multiple brand new games and it was quickly overwhelmed. Totally worthless. They did issue an immediate return label and refunded as soon as I shipped it back. Don’t buy this! 

It’s only been a month since I’ve purchased the Civic . So far it has exceeded my expectations, great ride , comfortable and great gas mileage. I have the 1.5 liter turbocharged engine and it’s great to drive! 

First off the electronics steering is lifeless.  Second it was misaligned from the factory and after two alignments it is still not corrected. The automatic braking and active cruise control cannot be disengaged even though they claim to be disengaged. If you’re going around the corner and there is a car in front of you in the next lane the car will slam on the brakes as if you were about to rear end someone and could potentially cause a crash.  This has happened to me three times and I can get the same results consistently now.   The interior looks nice but feels cheap, and the seats are horribly uncomfortable. 
"""

#Load the positive words dictionary from a file, and store the positive words in a list. 
pos_dict = []
f = open("C:\\cs621\\positive_words_list.txt")
f.seek(0)
for line in f:
    w = line.strip()
    pos_dict.append(w)
f.close()

f = open("C:\\cs621\\negative_words_list.txt")
f.seek(0)
for line in f:
    w = line.strip()
    neg_dict.append(w)
f.close()

for i in range(len(comments)):
    comments[i] = comments[i].translate(str.maketrans('','', string.punctuation))

In [18]:
positive_score  = 0
negative_score = 0
matched_pos_words = []
comment_words = comments[0].split()
for w in comment_words:
    w = w.lower()
    if w in pos_dict:
        positive_score = positive_score + 1
        matched_pos_words.append(w)
    if w in neg_dict:
        negative_score = negative_score + 1
print(positive_score)
print(negative_score)

for ent in doc.ents:
    print(ent.text, ent.label_)
